SHA256 8dbb7ee96ff481d8860c0caa687d08bcd21872ffbcb4537cac26da74b9e88424
    18    Its G.mc function, separately, only samples collectgarbage('count').
    19  - Widget refresh calls the same G.run once initialized. Its original background
    20    callback checks WGT.APP.initDone, then calls APP.G.ML.bg. This check is in the
    21    actual bytecode; it is not a decompiler invention. M exposes G.initDone, so do
    22    not assume that this optional widget background path is active.
    23  
    24  MAIN/ML original source lines
    25  - 178: MSP is allowed only when FC_MSP_ON, TLM_ON, and not ARMED.
    26  - 180: on becoming allowed, initialize startAt to now + START_DELAY (default 100
    27    ticks). Becoming disallowed clears startAt.
    28  - 187-196: after that delay, execute FC negotiation and MSPML as applicable.
    29  - 201: call ML.bg, which polls normal EdgeTX telemetry values.

    72   function c:tick(now,enabled,linkCurrent,disarmed)
    73    if not enabled then cancel('OFF');return s end
    74    if not transport or type(transport.push)~='function' or type(transport.pop)~='function' then cancel('UNAVAILABLE');return s end
    75    if linkCurrent~=true then cancel('NO CURRENT LINK');return s end
    76    if disarmed~=true then cancel('ARMED / UNKNOWN');return s end
    77    if lastTick and now<lastTick then cancel('CLOCK RESET')end
    78    lastTick=now
    79    if not s.active then s.active=true;s.status='STARTING';sendAt=now+startDelay end
    80    if blocked then return s end
    81    -- A strict per-callback budget avoids a Lua-queue drain monopolizing the UI.
    82    for _=1,maxPops do
    83     local kind,data=transport.pop()
    84     if kind==nil then break end
    85     receive(now,kind,data)
    86    end
    87    if blocked then return s end
    88    if pending and now-pending.sentAt>=responseWait then
    89     s.timeouts=s.timeouts+1;pending=nil;rx=nil;s.pendingCommand=nil;s.status='RESPONSE TIMEOUT';sendAt=now+interval
    90    end

   257   local function save()

   264    for i,id in ipairs(list)do if id==section then index=i end end

   358  function fcLink.update(now)
   359   if not fcLink.client then return end
   360   local rc,fc=configuration.get('rcProtocol'),configuration.get('fcType')
   361   local enabled=configuration.get('mspMode')==1 and fcLink.native and
   362    (rc=='None' or rc=='ELRS / CRSF' or rc=='TBS Crossfire') and (fc=='None' or fc=='Betaflight')

   510  local function bearing(x,y)
   511   local xx,yy=-y,-x
   512   if xx>0 then return math.atan(yy/xx)
   513   elseif xx<0 then return math.atan(yy/xx)+(yy>=0 and pi or -pi)
   514   elseif yy~=0 then return yy>=0 and pi/2 or -pi/2 end
   515   return 0
   516  end

   522  local function gpsPosition(v)
   523   if type(v)=='table' and type(v.lat)=='number' and type(v.lon)=='number' and v.lat==v.lat and v.lon==v.lon and math.abs(v.lat)<=90 and math.abs(v.lon)<=180 then return v end
   524  end
   525  local function atan2(y,x)
   526   if x>0 then return math.atan(y/x) elseif x<0 then return math.atan(y/x)+(y>=0 and pi or -pi) elseif y>0 then return pi/2 elseif y<0 then return -pi/2 end
   527   return 0
   528  end
   529  local function geo(a,b)
   530   local r=pi/180;local la,lb=a.lat*r,b.lat*r;local dl=(b.lon-a.lon)*r;local dp=lb-la
   531   local h=clamp(math.sin(dp/2)^2+math.cos(la)*math.cos(lb)*math.sin(dl/2)^2,0,1)
   532   local dist=6371000*2*atan2(math.sqrt(h),math.sqrt(1-h))
   533   local angle=(atan2(math.sin(dl)*math.cos(lb),math.cos(la)*math.sin(lb)-math.sin(la)*math.cos(lb)*math.cos(dl))/r+360)%360
   534   return dist,angle
   535  end

    37    local angle = (gc2 - gc1) % 360
    38    return angle
    39  end
    40  function D.GPS.calcDistance(c1, c2)
    41    if c1 == nil or c2 == nil then
    42      return
    43    end
    44    local lat1 = math.rad(c1.lat)
    45    local lat2 = math.rad(c2.lat)
    46    local lon2 = math.rad(c2.lon)
    47    local a = math.sin((lat2 - lat1) / 2) ^ 2 + math.sin((lon2 - math.rad(c1.lon)) / 2) ^ 2 * math.cos(lat1) * math.cos(lat2)
    48    local c = 2 * math.atan(math.sqrt(a), math.sqrt(1 - a))
    49    local d = G.round(c * D.GPS.EARTH_R, 0)
    50    return d
    51  end

    57    local ostime = D.TI.now

    59      D.GPS.posLog[logID] = {date = date, time = time, ostime = ostime, lat = lat, lon = lon}

     9    if D.S.BT_MD_V then
    10      if D.S.BT_MD_V >= D.S.BT_CELL_NOPWR then
    11        for c = 1, 6 do
    12          if D.S.BT_MD_V >= D.S.BT_CELL_MAX * (c - 1) then
    13            if D.S.BT_MD_V <= D.S.BT_CELL_MAX * c then
    14              cells = c
    15              break
    16            end
    17          end
    18        end
    19        cells = math.max(math.min(cells, 6), 1)
    20      end
    21    end
    22    return cells
